|| 42 || 43 ||

Now he explains another point: **"Starting in the empty..."** If a person moves from the "Empty" position <note>The side where the nostril is not currently breathing.</note> and sits in the "Full" position <note>The side where the breath is actively flowing.</note> during a question, then all their goals will be achieved. In questions regarding life and death, profit and loss, or victory and defeat, success is certain. In life inquiries, they will live; in death inquiries, they will die; in profit inquiries, they will gain profit; against an enemy, they will achieve victory. All this should be understood by us.

Regarding the term "Inverse" <note>original: "vame" or "vamam," literally "left" but here meaning "contrary" or "opposite."</note>: If one starts in the "Full" position and then moves to and sits in the "Empty" position, then one should predict the "Inverse" result. This means life results in death, death in life, profit in loss, and victory in defeat. This is the meaning of the word "Inverse." || 44 ||

Now, regarding pregnancy:

---

> Rogues, rivals, masters, and thieves, and the five relatives; || when they stand in the Full position, they bring results for the womb. || 42 || If the breath is in the Sun <note>The right nostril.</note>, the movement is uneven; if in the Moon <note>The left nostril.</note>, it is even. || If the messenger speaks from the outside <note>The "Empty" side.</note>, then the result is otherwise, not as expected. || 43 ||
> 
> If one first goes to the "Empty" side and then enters the "Full" side, || then one should know for certain that all goals will be achieved. || 44 || In a question of pregnancy, if the messenger is in the "Full" position, || a son is born. In the "Empty," a girl. If there is a transition <note>A change in breath flow during the query.</note>, there are twins; and if the transition occurs exactly at the moment of the sun's entry into a new sign, a miscarriage occurs. || 45 ||

---

### Now he explains the strength of the breath regarding inquiries about pregnancy

**"In a question of pregnancy..."** When the breath flows in the left or right, or moves upward, etc., and the messenger stays in the "Full" position, one should say there is a pregnancy and whether it will be a son or daughter. When the breath is "Full," a son will be born. If it goes to the "Empty," then one should say a daughter will be born. 

Regarding twins: If the breath is first in the "Full" and then moves to the "Empty," one should predict twins—either two boys, or a boy and then a girl. If it is first "Empty" and then becomes "Full," then it is a daughter first and then a son. This is the power of one who practices the movement of the breath! This is not understood merely by reading. 

Regarding miscarriage: If the breath moves—whether in the left or the right—from "Full" to another state at the moment of "Transition" <term>Samkramana: The specific moment the breath shifts or the Sun enters a new zodiac sign</term>, one should declare either the death of the mother or a miscarriage for the one asking about the pregnancy. || 45 ||

---

> If the breath flows in the Earth or Water elements, she stays in comfort. || If it goes into the Air element, she goes elsewhere; in the Fire element, one should declare her dead. || 46 || If the sunrise aligns with the path of the Moon <note>Left nostril.</note> and they are equal, || then there is a gathering of good qualities; if they are reversed, there is confusion. || 47 ||

---

### Now regarding inquiries about the arrival of others

**"If she comes..."** Now, when a question is asked about someone who has gone away, one should judge the good or bad results by the "Full" and "Empty" states, and then speak of their arrival or return. 

If the breath flows in the Earth or Water elements, then the traveler will arrive. If the breath is in the Earth element, say: "They stay there in comfort." If it flows in the **Air element**, the traveler has gone elsewhere. If it flows in the **Fire element**, one should say there is a struggle or a fire. Here too, one should use the signs of "Full" and "Empty" to predict good or bad outcomes, such as a potential death. || 46 ||

Now he explains the result of the union of the two nostrils: **"The sunrise and the path..."** If the sunrise occurs when the left breath is flowing, it is auspicious. If the sunset occurs while that same breath is flowing, then a "gathering of qualities" (success) occurs. If it is reversed, there is confusion. If the Sun rises when the breath is in the right nostril and sets while in the left, or if the sunrise and sunset both occur in the same nostril improperly, it leads to confusion and lack of success. || 47 ||

<summary>This page details how the "Full" and "Empty" states of the nostrils predict success in general life, the gender of a child, or the safety of a traveler. It emphasizes that a shift from the inactive to the active side during a query is a sign of certain success.</summary>

Now another point is explained regarding the "fortnight" <note>original: paksha; in this context, it refers to the breath remaining stuck in one nostril for a period of fifteen days.</note> in relation to the left and right breath flow. Here, it is easily understood that clarity of mind occurs when the moon <note>the left nostril breath</note> rises. If one gains a profit but then abandons the natural flow and relies on the opposite side—meaning the breath crosses over from its proper side—then illness will occur. If it stays on one side for two fortnights, it leads to the loss of desired objects. If it stays for three fortnights, death follows. || 47 ||

Now he speaks of the knowledge of the elements <note>original: tattvas</note> and their divisions. Whether in the left or the right: when the **Earth element** flows, it extends eight finger-widths <note>original: angula; a traditional unit of measurement</note> from the nostril. In that state, one should perform their duties. When the flow is sixteen finger-widths, it is the **Water element**. When it flows from the nostrils and reaches the tip of the nose or stays within a boundary of eight finger-widths <note>this appears to be a specific diagnostic measurement</note>, that is the Earth element. By observing others in this way, knowledge arises. || 48 ||

Again, regarding questions asked by a petitioner, he describes the nature of the elements:

**If the breath exceeds one fortnight, disease arises; if two fortnights, the loss of desired things.**
**Exceeding three fortnights, death certainly occurs. || 48 ||**
**In either the left or the right, Earth is eight finger-widths long. ||**
**Water is sixteen finger-widths; Fire is four finger-widths. || 49 ||**
**Air is twelve finger-widths long; Ether has no [length]. || 49 ||**
**Earth is yellow; Water is white; Fire is the color of the Fire-God <note>original: Dhananjayah; a name for fire</note>, red. || 50 ||**
**Ether is the color of a dark blue cloud; these are the five colors. || 50 ||**
**A yellow square with the syllable "Lam" in the middle is the supreme Earth mandala. ||**
**A white, bow-shaped <note>crescent</note> six-petaled form is the Water mandala. || 51 ||**

Now he describes the colors of Earth and the other elements. "Earth is yellow"—this means that when a question is asked, one should describe the color associated with the dominant element; this is easy to understand. || 50 ||

Now the teacher explains the different names and divisions of the five elements. The "yellow square" and so on are the forms of the five elements as proclaimed in the sacred traditions <note>original: Agamas</note>. Through these colors, one perceives the world. Here, regarding the five elements like Earth, one should apply the inner wisdom of the "triple secret" <note>original: kile shatrayaneti; likely referring to the integration of breath, mantra, and form</note> to each form.

In this context, when a question is asked regarding anxieties or other matters:
1. If the **Earth element** is active, one should speak of the yellow square and the sound "Lam."
2. For the **Water element**, one should speak of the white, six-sided <note>or six-petaled</note> form and the sound "Vam."
3. For the **Fire element**, one should speak of the red triangle.
4. For the **Air element**, one should speak of the eight-angled green or smoke-colored form.
5. For the **Ether element**, one should speak of the smoke-colored circle.

Furthermore, wherever a particular thought or inspiration strikes the mind, one should use logic to provide the answer to the question. Other knowers of Yoga say that the Moon and Sun breaths <note>left and right nostrils</note> exist in the bodies of all living beings. The Moon is below, the Sun is said to be above <note>likely referring to their energetic positions in the subtle body</note>; one should avoid the "Empty" times. In women, the Moon represents the female organ <note>original: yoni</note>, and in men, the Sun is spoken of as the male. These two are undoubtedly the cause of creation and destruction for all beings. This is easy to understand and not excessively difficult for practical use. || 51 ||

<summary>This page details the diagnostic lengths of breath associated with the five elements (Earth, Water, Fire, Air, and Ether) and provides the specific colors and geometric shapes used for divination. It also warns that breath remaining in a single nostril for several weeks is an omen of illness or death.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the technical instructions on the *Svarodaya* (Science of Breath), specifically concluding the second chapter of the "Movement of the Swan" (*Hansachara*). It transitions from the physical measurements of the breath-elements (*tattvas*) to their visual characteristics and the specific activities appropriate for the "Sun" (right) and "Moon" (left) breath flows.</meta>

The **Fire realm** <note>original: vahni-mandalam; visualized as the element of fire within the breath</note> is red and shaped like a triangle. The **Air realm** <note>original: vayu-mandalam</note> is green and situated within an eight-pointed <note>original: ashta-patram; literally "eight-petaled"</note> shape. || 52 ||

In the center is the **Ether realm** <note>original: vyoma-mandalam; the element of space or void</note>, which is circular and has the color of smoke. These five elements have been declared auspicious by the ancient sages of the past. || 53 ||

Destructive acts <note>original: marana; "killing"</note>, deluding others, paralyzing an opponent, creating enmity, expelling enemies, subjugation, inciting others, attraction, and causing agitation should be performed when the **Sun** <note>the breath flowing through the right nostril</note> is active. || 54 ||

Acts of peace, nourishing activities, the preparation of divine medicines and elixirs <note>original: rasayane; traditional Indian alchemy or rejuvenation therapy</note>, and the practice of Yoga should always be performed when the **Moon** <note>the breath flowing through the left nostril</note> is active. || 55 ||

Those men who always act in accordance with the flow of the Moon and Sun breaths will always have the knowledge of the past and the future within their hands. || 56 ||

Thus ends the section on the *Svarodaya*.

### Thus ends the second chapter, titled "The Movement of the Swan," in the *Svarodaya* of the *Shri Brahmayamala*. || 2 ||

The variations mentioned in the five breaths are either natural or produced by time. They exist in the stages of childhood, youth, old age, and even death. One should understand these through the method of the Guru's instruction, relying on the primary and secondary divisions. Those who know this certainly understand the good and bad fortunes of their family across the three periods of time <note>past, present, and future</note>. || 1 ||

--- Page 90 ---
<meta>The previous page (28) contained a commentary on the Amarakośa lexicon. However, this page marks a significant shift in subject matter, beginning a treatise on the Sarvatobhadra Chakra, a complex astrological grid used for divination and mapping celestial influences. This suggests the manuscript is a compendium of various Vedic sciences.</meta>

### Now, therefore, I shall fully explain...

Now then, in the sense of succession following the "Gait of the Swan" <note>original: hamsachara; likely the title of the preceding chapter on breath or movement</note>, I shall fully explain the chart named "Auspicious from All Sides" <note>original: Sarvatobhadra-nama-chakra</note>, which is distinguished as the "Illuminator of the Three Worlds" <note>original: Trailokya-dipakam</note>. || 9 ||

It illuminates—that is, it makes manifest—the three worlds. Just as a lamp reveals a pot, this "Illuminator of the Three Worlds" is famous and established in the world. By the light and space it provides, it immediately produces conviction <note>original: pratyaya-karakam</note>. When contemplated, it acts as the indicator of good and bad fortune. || 1 ||

Now, he speaks of the placement of the chart. The instruction to "Place ten [lines] across" is easy to understand. || 2 ||

Sitting with the face toward the East, one should draw the chart starting with the letter 'A'. In the eye-markers <note>the squares or points of the grid</note> the vowels, beginning with 'A', are to be written. The sixteen vowels should be written, and the lunar mansions beginning with Krittika should be placed in the intermediate directions starting with the Northeast <note>original: Isha; the direction ruled by Ishana/Shiva</note>. 

The sequence is as follows: The vowel **A** in the Northeast corner; **Ā** in the Southeast; **I** in the Southwest; **Ī** in the Northwest; **U** back in the Northeast corner; **Ū** below it; **A** below that in the Southeast; **Ā** below that. In this manner, the sixteen vowels are placed starting from the Northeast direction.

---

> Now, therefore, I shall fully explain the chart that illuminates the three worlds, known as the "Auspicious from All Sides," which brings immediate results. || 1 ||
>
> Having drawn ten horizontal lines and ten vertical lines, a chart of eighty-one squares is produced; of this, there is no doubt. || 2 ||
>
> The vowels beginning with **A** should be placed in the squares of the intermediate directions starting with the Northeast. These sixteen should be placed according to the "Path of Creation" <note>original: srishti-marga; usually meaning clockwise or in the order of manifestation</note> in four rotations. || 3 ||
>
> One should write the lunar mansions <note>term: Nakshatra; the 27 or 28 divisions of the sky the moon passes through</note> beginning with Krittika, starting from the Northeast in sequence. Seven by seven in order, totaling twenty-eight <note>This includes Abhijit, the "intercalary" 28th mansion</note>. || 4 ||
>
> The groups of letters beginning with **A-Va-Ka-Ha-Da** are in the East (eight), and those in the South are **Ma-Ta-Pa-Ra-Ta**. The **Na-Ya-Ma-Bha-Ja-Kha** groups are in the West, and **Ga-Ma-Da-Cha-La** are in the North. || 5 ||
>
> The groups of three beginning with the North, such as **Va-Ga-Sha**, should be placed by the wise in order. The zodiac signs <note>term: Rashi; the twelve signs of the solar zodiac like Aries or Leo</note> should be placed in the ten... following the Path of Creation. || 6 ||

---

One should place these in the squares. While writing the vowels in this way, there will be four rotations following the Path of Creation, ending the writing of vowels.

Now, after writing the vowels, he speaks of writing the lunar mansions. Regarding the instruction for "Krittika and the rest," one should write seven in each of the four directions in the squares around the vowel-squares. For instance: seven in the first row of the East (7); below that, seven in the southern row (7); and below that in the western square; and similarly in the northern square. The squares are spoken of in the four directions. 

Starting from the eye-marker, one should write Krittika and the others. One should write the seven starting with Magha in the South. One should write the seven starting with Maitra <note>original: Maitra; another name for the constellation Anuradha</note> in the West. One should write the seven starting with Dhanishtha in the North. || 4 ||

Now, he speaks of writing in the squares below the letters. The five letters **A-Va-Ka-Ha-Da** should be written in the five squares below the lunar mansions in the East. The five letters **Ma-Ta-Pa-Ra-Ta** should be written in the five squares in the South below the lunar mansions. The letters **Na-Ya-Ma-Bha-Ja-Kha** should be written in the five squares in the West below the lunar mansions. The five letters **Ga-Ma-Da-Cha-La** should be written in the squares below the northern lunar mansions. || 5 ||

He speaks of what is to be written in the triangles of the second row starting from the East. The three zodiac signs starting with Vrishabha <note>Taurus</note> should be written in the eastern square. The three starting with Simha <note>Leo</note> should be written in the southern square. The three starting with Vrischika <note>Scorpio</note> should be written in the western square. The three starting with Kumbha <note>Aquarius</note> should be written in the northern squares. || 6 ||

Now he speaks of what is to be written in the remaining parts. Regarding the "remaining," in the four directions already mentioned, in each of the four squares and in the single middle square, one should write the five-fold movement-diagram <note>original: gati-yantra</note> of the Sun and others in sequence.

<meta>This page continues the technical instructions for constructing the Sarvatobhadra Chakra (Auspicious from All Sides Chart). <margin>
Na-Ga <note>Likely an abbreviation for a section or specific branch of the text</note>
Ja. Ti. <note>Likely an abbreviation for "Janma-tithi" or a specific commentary title</note>
27
</margin>

<margin>27</margin>
The first lunar day <note>term: pratipat; the first day after the new or full moon</note> should be written in the eastern cell. The second should be written in the southern cell, the third in the western, the fourth in the northern, and the fifth in the center. In this way, the five groups of days—beginning with "Joyous" <note>term: Nanda; refers to the 1st, 6th, and 11th lunar days</note>—are to be written according to their entry. 

Similarly, the five-day groups beginning with the eleventh day should be written according to their entry. By doing this, the groups of five, ten, and fifteen days are written within the three circuits. Regarding the weekdays: beginning with Mars, the seven weekdays should be written, or rather, written in sequence alongside the "Joyous" <note>Nanda</note> and other lunar days. In those cells where the "Joyous" and other days are written, one should also write Mars and the others. There, in the same cell as Mars, the "Joyous" days are written; in the "Prosperous" <note>term: Bhadra; 2nd, 7th, 12th days</note> cell, Mercury should be written; in the "Victorious" <note>term: Jaya; 3rd, 8th, 13th days</note> cell, Jupiter; in the "Empty" <note>term: Rikta; 4th, 9th, 14th days</note> cell, Venus; and in the "Full" <note>term: Purna; 5th, 10th, 15th days</note> cell, Saturn. Furthermore, the Moon should be written with Mars, and Mercury in the "Prosperous" cell. By writing the weekdays in this manner, the "Auspicious from All Sides" mandala is formed.

> Thus the poet says: In these cells, the five-fold lunar days beginning with "Joyous" and the seven-fold weekdays beginning with Mars and the Sun are to be written. || 7 || 
> 
> Mars and the Sun are placed in the "Joyous" day cell; Mercury and the Moon in the "Prosperous"; Jupiter in the "Victorious"; and Venus in the "Empty." || 8 || 
> 
> Saturn is to be written in the "Full" day cell once the chart is constructed. This is the extension of the "Auspicious from All Sides" chart as proclaimed by me. || 9 || 
> 
> A partial sight <note>original: ardha-drishti</note> of Mars and the Sun occurs in cells 3 and 9; for Mercury, it is 8 and 4, looking downward. || 10 || 
> 
> One should observe the upward gaze of a planet in a "debilitated" position and the downward gaze of one in an "exalted" position. Aspect <note>drishti</note> is categorized in three ways: across the same row, to the side, and diagonal. || 11 || 
> 
> Saturn, the Sun, and Rahu are considered "cruel" <note>term: krura; malefic</note>; the remaining are "benefic" <note>shubha</note> planets. Mercury is cruel when associated with a malefic or when its power is waning. || 12 || 
> 
> From the circle where a planet is situated, a "threefold piercing" <note>term: vedha; an astrological interference</note> occurs. In this system, the "piercing" of a planet depends on whether it is to the left, in front, or to the right. || 13 ||

This is how it is formed. Thus the poet says: "This is the extension of the 'Auspicious from All Sides' chart as proclaimed by me." This follows the entry sequence and is easily understood according to the *Brahmayamala* <note>A foundational Tantric text often cited for astrological and ritual instructions</note>. || 7 || 8 || 9 || 10 ||

<margin>॥१९॥</margin> Now, he explains the division of "cruel" planets for the consideration of the chart. The phrase "Saturn, Sun, and Rahu" is easy to understand. || 12 || 

Next, he speaks of the "piercing" <note>vedha</note> inquiry through the aspect of the planets. He says: From the lunar mansion where a planet is situated, there is a "threefold piercing" through the planet's gaze. For the great soul, this gaze is three-fold: one gaze pierces directly in front, one pierces to the right, and one pierces to the left. || 13 ||

One should enjoy pleasures and the beloved, just as one takes the hand in marriage. However, in auspicious works, one must avoid [these specific alignments] with great effort. || 114 || When a planet is in retrograde <note>term: vakra; appearing to move backward in the sky</note>, its "piercing" gaze <note>term: vedha; an astrological aspect or influence</note> is to the right. When in fast motion <note>term: shighra; moving faster than its average speed</note>, its gaze is to the left. In average motion, its gaze is known to be centered. This is how the effects of Mars and other planets are understood within the chart. || 115 ||

Verse 114 [above] explains that according to the three types of sight, there are three types of "piercing." Verse 115 explains the specific characteristics:

The five planets—beginning with Mars and Saturn <note>original: yamo bhoma; Yama refers to Saturn</note>—each possess three distinct types of sight. When a planet is retrograde, it is "vakra." When it moves toward its highest point <note>term: uccha; exaltation</note>, it is "shighra" (fast-moving). One must understand that the three types of "piercing" come from these three types of sight.

A special rule is noted here: a planet "pierces" the target directly across, whether the path is straight or diagonal. It "pierces" letters, star-houses <note>term: nakshatras</note>, and specific sounds through the face-to-face alignment without any obstacle. Some teachers say that in this state of equality, one should look at the four-fold groups of vowels and other elements. By facing them, the planet "pierces" them immediately. In this regard, the following verses describe the "piercing" using the "Five-Pair" system: for example, the Sun <note>original: pāvakaḥ; literally 'the purifier,' a name for the Sun or Fire</note> destroys the stars Ashwini, Rohini, Hasta, and Abhijit. The moon's nodes and other planets also affect groups of five stars situated in front of them. All this must be understood systematically. || 115 ||

# N. J. || J. J. T. || || 28 ||
<margin>28</margin>
<note>The header abbreviations "N. J." and "J. J. T." likely refer to the *Navagraha Jataka* and its *Teeka* (commentary).</note>

Now, the teacher explains that without understanding the motions of the planets <note>term: graha-gati; the specific speed and direction of a planet</note>, the "great results" cannot be determined. Without defining the characteristics of slow, fast, and retrograde motions, their majesty is lost. First, the planets move through the circle of rays to determine the time. Then, relative to the Sun's position:

In the second house from the Sun, the planets are "fast-moving" <note>original: "shighra-ga"; moving faster than their average daily motion</note>. In the third house from the Sun, they are "equal" <note>original: "sama"; moving at their mean or average speed</note>. When the planets are in the fourth house from the Sun, they become "slow" <note>original: "manda"; moving slower than average</note>.

In the fifth, sixth, and seventh houses, the planets become "retrograde" <note>term: vakra; an optical illusion where a planet appears to move backward against the stars</note>. Specifically, Mars and the other four planets <note>Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, and Saturn</note> are retrograde in the fifth and sixth houses. Some say that according to their distance from the Sun, they are "extremely retrograde" <note>original: "ati-vakra"</note> when in the seventh house. In the ninth and tenth houses, their motion is "crooked" <note>original: "kutila"</note>. In the eleventh and twelfth houses from the Sun, they become "fast-moving" again. By means of these retrograde, fast, and medium motions, the three-fold "aspect" <note>term: drishti; the "glance" or influence a planet casts on other signs</note> of the planets occurs.

Therefore:
A planet is said to be "rising" when released by the Sun; when gripped by the Sun, it is "setting." **||**
Mars and the others are "fast" when in the second house from the Sun. **|| 16 ||**
They are known as "equal" in the third house, and "slow" in the fourth from the Sun. **||**
They are "retrograde" in the fifth and sixth, and "extremely retrograde" in the seventh. **|| 17 ||**
In the ninth and tenth from the Sun, a "crooked" motion is born. **||**
In the twelfth and eleventh, they again enjoy "fast" motion. **||**
In this world, these eight states of motion are born from the Sun's position. **|| 18 ||**

<term>Rahu and Ketu</term> <note>The North and South Nodes of the Moon, respectively; they are shadow planets responsible for eclipses</note> are always retrograde. The Moon and Sun are always fast-moving. **||**
Because of the singular nature of their movement, their "aspect" is always constant. **|| 19 ||**
Cruel planets <note>original: "krura"; malefics like Mars or Saturn</note> in retrograde become "extremely cruel." Auspicious planets <note>original: "saumya"; benefics like Jupiter or Venus</note> in retrograde become "extremely auspicious." **||**
Benefics and malefics in their natural state or in fast motion behave according to their inherent nature. **|| 20 ||**

---

**Regarding the Aspects, etc. || 16 || 17 || 18 || Now, regarding special motions, he describes the specific motions: ||**

"Rahu and Ketu are always retrograde"—this means that Rahu and Ketu always move in a retrograde direction. The Moon and the Sun are only fast-moving. These four—Sun, Moon, Rahu, and Ketu—do not have varying natures of motion <note>Sun/Moon never go retrograde; Nodes never go direct</note>; therefore, they watch with a constant, direct gaze. Rahu and Ketu's gaze is considered "retrograde" because of their constant retrograde nature. <note>The text here discusses technical nuances of how the "gaze" or vedha of Rahu and Ketu is calculated similarly to fast-moving planets.</note> **|| 19 ||**

Regarding "Cruel planets in retrograde": If auspicious planets are joined with or aspected by benefics, they remain so; however, if cruel planets <note>malefics</note> are retrograde, they become exceedingly cruel. If auspicious planets <note>benefics</note> are retrograde, they become "greatly auspicious." **||** Benefics in fast motion...

<meta>This page continues the technical instructions for the Sarvatobhadra Chakra. It transitions from describing planetary speeds (retrograde and stationing) to the specific rules of "vedha" (astrological piercing or aspecting) involving vowels, specific consonants, and the directional placement of lunar mansions (nakshatras).</meta>

When planets reach a state of stationing <note>term: stambha; a point where a planet appears to stand still before changing direction</note>, they reside in their natural disposition. Those in their natural disposition quickly become powerful. They act according to their inherent nature. However, when they are retrograde <note>term: vakra; appearing to move backward</note>, they act differently. In that state, even malefic planets <note>original: "krura"; literally 'cruel' or 'harsh' planets like Saturn or Mars</note> that are moving fast in their own nature become fruit-givers. According to the rules of birth astrology <note>term: Jataka; individual horoscopy</note>, if benefic planets <note>original: "shubha"; auspicious planets like Jupiter or Venus</note> are retrograde, they grant kingdoms. If malefic planets are retrograde, they cause vices and suffering. Furthermore, during their planetary periods <note>term: dasha; a time-block in a person's life ruled by a specific planet</note>, they cause one to wander here and there. || 20 || <insert>Now he describes the piercing (vedha).</insert>

Regarding the vowels here: the vowels are joined with the nasal sound <note>term: anusvara; the 'm' sound marked by a dot above a letter</note> and the aspirated sound <note>term: visarga; the 'h' sound marked by two dots after a letter</note>. Through their "piercing," the connection of both should be understood. Since these are uttered as part of the "N-sound" group, it is impossible to pronounce them without the vowel 'a'. || 21 || Now, he explains the specifics of the "piercing" of letters (varna). <insert>Now, in the chart, the 'p-group' and the letter 'va' are mentioned.</insert> <insert>The 'y-group' and the letter 'sa' are mentioned. If the 's-group', 'sha', and 'sa' belong to anyone...</insert>

In this vowel-piercing, one vowel affects two. When a vowel is joined with the self, the piercing of the nasal sound (anusvara) and aspirated sound (visarga) occurs. || 22 || The groups of letters beginning with 'va', as well as 'sha', 'sha', 'sa', and 'kha', should be known... || 21 || One planet may pierce two letters in the calculation of auspicious or inauspicious results. || 22 || <insert>The letter 'gha' and the number 5 should be known by their angle...</insert> <insert>...as previously stated here in the story.</insert> Thus, the three-by-three dots should be placed one by one in order. || 23 || <insert>The letter 'gha' (5) and the upward piercing...</insert> <insert>...as before, these are in the north.</insert> || 24 || In this vowel-piercing, when one vowel is pierced, two are affected. This is not the case in the piercing of other vowels. 
->|| The nasal sound (anusvara) and aspirated sound (visarga) || 25 ||<-

---

In name-piercing, the letter 'va' belongs to the 'p-group'; the palatal 'sha' belongs to its own group; the 'va' belonging to the 'k-group' should be known. Their piercing is understood through the general rules of piercing already mentioned. || 22 || <insert>The letters 'gha', 5, and 'sha'...</insert> <insert>These letters like 'gha' and 'da' are placed in the vowel-slots of the lunar mansions (nakshatras). 'Gha', 5, and 'da'...</insert> <insert>...the mansions of Magha, Ashlesha, Purva-Phalguni, Hasta, Purva-Ashadha, and Uttara-Bhadrapada. Between them are the letters 'la' and 'va'. Regarding the corners, in the piercing of these, when a planet is situated in the last quarter (pada) of a mansion:
The piercing of the four starting with the vowel is full and complete in its order. || 26 ||

Specifically: in the Northeast corner <note>original: "Ishan-kona"</note>, the triad of Bharani, etc. In the Southeast <note>original: "Agneya"</note>, Magha and Ashlesha. In the Southwest <note>original: "Nairutya"</note>, Vishakha and Anuradha. In the Northwest <note>original: "Vayu"</note>, Shravana and Dhanishtha. For those moving in the last quarters of these pairs, a piercing of the four corner positions arises. Likewise, the piercing of the "Full" lunar days <note>term: Purna Tithi; the 5th, 10th, and 15th days of the lunar fortnight</note> occurs. In the quarters of Bharani and the triad, the planet pierces the vowels 'a', 'u', and 'l'. In Ashlesha and Magha, it pierces 'a', 'u', and 'l'. In Vishakha and Anuradha, it pierces 'i', 'ri', 'e', and 'o'. In Shravana and Dhanishtha, it pierces 'ee', 'ree', 'ai', and 'au'. The corner positions also pierce the "Full" lunar days.

<meta>This page continues the technical instructions for the Sarvatobhadra Chakra. <margin>29</margin>
**Now, another explanation** regarding the equal piercing of the lunar mansions <note>term: nakshatra; the 27 or 28 divisions of the sky through which the Moon passes</note> and other elements. The vowels Ṛ, Ḷ, Ā, I, Ī, E, and Ai are pierced in groups of four. Since piercing by a single letter is impossible, the piercing of vowels like ‘U’ occurs in conjunction with the groups <note>term: varga; categorized groups of consonants in the Sanskrit alphabet</note> and signs. This is the explanation for the piercing of these specific sounds. **|| 26 ||**

**Now he states the results of the piercing:** Through the piercing of one or more malefic planets <note>original: "krura"; literally 'cruel' or 'harsh' planets</note>—namely Saturn, the Sun, Rahu, Ketu, and Mars—these five are called the "sinners" <note>term: papa-graha; malefic planets that bring difficult karma</note>. When any one of them pierces a person's name, vowel, group, sign, or lunar mansion, agitation <note>term: udvega; a state of mental distress or anxiety</note> is born. They become troubled in mind. **With the piercing of two planets, fear arises. || 22 <insert>sic. 27</insert> ||** And from one’s own chart or from any other source, fear is produced. **With the piercing of two houses <note>original: "griha"; houses or signs in the chart</note>, there is ruin.** A king’s rise or a battle may lead to fleeing. In a dispute over wealth, there is a **loss of wealth.** In a dispute over knowledge, there is a **loss of learning.** One should observe the effects in this manner. The calculation of one, two, or more piercings applies to vowels, groups, and signs as well. **The Consideration of Planetary Piercing:** In the consideration of the vowels, groups, lunar mansions, and signs, the malefic planets determine the results of agitation and so on. If one is strong among these, agitation occurs through one planet; with the piercing of multiple planets, great agitation occurs. Thus, the number of planets

---

> Through the piercing of one or more malefic planets, results are produced for men:
> Agitation, delusion, loss, disease, and death, in that order. **|| 27 ||**
> When the lunar mansion is pierced, there is delusion; when a letter is pierced, there is loss;
> When a vowel is pierced, there is disease and fear.
> When a zodiac sign is pierced, there is great calamity; when all five elements are pierced, one does not live. **|| 28 ||**
> One piercing brings fear in battle; a pair of piercings causes loss of wealth;
> Three piercings result in disease; and death occurs when there is a set of four. **|| 29 ||**

---

determines the variation of the agitation. Likewise, based on the piercing of the letters and vowels by two or more planets, one should also predict fear and ruin. Similarly, regarding gains, one should speak of more or less depending on the piercing of the planets in the directions of the vowels' strength. **|| 27 ||** He explains the consideration of agitation and other effects in this piercing: regarding "delusion in the lunar mansion," the piercing of a lunar mansion by <insert>one or more</insert> planets causes delusion, which is a lack of knowledge/clarity. That itself is agitation; indeed, agitation leads to delusion. A piercing of a "letter" <note>term: varna; a specific consonant or character in the person's name</note> causes loss—meaning loss of work, business, or possessions. Disease occurs when a "vowel" is pierced. Thus, disease is mentioned when four malefic planets pierce. When a vowel is struck by four, one is overcome by disease. Here is the consideration: while agitation and so on are mentioned for one or more piercings, these manifest in the person. When four planets pierce the vowel, the results ranging from agitation to disease occur. It was previously mentioned that a piercing of three brings fear; in the case of a couple, fear should be predicted when two malefic planets pierce. Here too, agitation and delusion occur. "Great calamity" in the sign refers to a great catastrophe or death. It is later stated that death occurs from the piercing of five planets. Here, "piercing in the sign" does not mean that alone; it means that through the piercing of five malefic planets in the sign, the indication is death. "With five piercings one does not live" means that when all five elements are pierced, the death of the subject occurs. This is the author’s intent: death results specifically from five planets piercing the zodiac sign. **|| 28 || Now he states the specifics:** "One piercing," etc. In this way, one should predict fear specifically in battle and not elsewhere. With the piercing of a pair of planets, there is loss of wealth. In three piercings, one flees from the battlefield. In the piercing of a fourth planet, death occurs; specifically, death in battle. Here too, one must consider the number of houses and the strength of the planets as described before. **|| 29 ||**

<vocab>
- Vedha: An astrological "piercing" or aspect that occurs between a planet and a specific point on the Sarvatobhadra grid.
- Graha: Planet; in this context, specifically the "malefics" that cause negative effects.
- Udvega: Agitation, anxiety, or mental distress.
- Nakshatra: A lunar mansion; one of the 27/28 divisions of the zodiac used to track the moon.
- Rashi: A zodiac sign (e.g., Aries, Taurus).
- Swara: A vowel; treated as a sensitive point in this alphabetical chart.
- Papagraha: Malefic or "sinful" planets (Sun, Mars, Saturn, Rahu, Ketu).
- Mrityu: Death; the ultimate negative result of multiple malefic aspects.
</vocab>

<summary>This page details the specific negative outcomes—ranging from mental agitation to death—that occur when malefic planets "pierce" (aspect) letters, vowels, or signs in the Sarvatobhadra Chakra. It provides a numerical hierarchy of suffering based on how many malefic planets are involved, with particular focus on outcomes in battle and disputes.</summary>

<keywords>vedha, malefic planets, Sarvatobhadra Chakra, agitation, battle omens, planetary aspect, lunar mansions, zodiac signs, Sanskrit astrology</keywords>

--- Page 96 ---
<meta>This page continues the technical analysis of the Sarvatobhadra Chakra. The previous page discussed how malefic planets "pierce" (aspect) specific vowels and sounds to create distress or ruin. This page expands on those effects, contrasting the results of malefic planets with benefic planets and introducing the method for calculating a planet's strength based on its zodiacal position.</meta>

Alternatively, even if a house <note>term: griha; a zodiac sign or house in the horoscope</note> indicates a fortunate result, the "piercing" <note>term: vedha; an astrological phenomenon where a planet's position affects or "strikes" a specific point on the Sarvatobhadra grid</note> might dictate otherwise. Just as a one-day piercing is said to produce results like agitation, one should also calculate the results for a one-day piercing in terms of gain, profit, and happiness. In this system, planets associated with malefic influences <note>original: "krura"; literally 'cruel' or harsh planets like Saturn or Mars</note> become malefic themselves and grant harsh results. Otherwise, a planet is auspicious. For example, a benefic planet becomes harsh if it is overcome by the Moon’s waning phase. Although it is not stated as an absolute rule here, one should determine the increase or decrease of effects based on the strength of the planet; if the malefic influence is greater, the suffering is greater, and if the benefic influence is greater, the success is greater. **|| 30 ||**

Just as the Lord stated that malefic planets cause harm to the body and so on, the Chakra also describes separate results for the different types of piercing. It is said that in the "Piercing of the Sun," there is mental distress. When the Sun <note>original: "Arka"</note> pierces a point, mental agony occurs; what this means is that agitation arises. Because the Sun’s lunar mansion <note>term: nakshatra; the 27 divisions of the sky through which the moon travels</note> is pierced, mental distress follows. Here, bodily harm is mentioned; therefore, when pierced by Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note>, the body is afflicted. When the sound is pierced, loss of wealth occurs. It was previously stated that "diseases come through the vowels"; therefore, when Saturn <note>original: "Shani"</note> pierces a vowel, diseases occur. When a zodiac sign <note>term: rashi</note> is pierced, great obstacles arise. Here, the North and South Nodes of the Moon <note>term: Rahu and Ketu</note> are the givers of obstacles.

<margin>31</margin>
->Just as malefic planets produce evil results, so do benefics <note>original: "saumya"; 'gentle' or benefic planets</note> provide auspicious results.<-
->However, benefics joined with malefics should be known to provide malefic results. **|| 30 ||**<-
->In the piercing of the Sun, there is mental distress; in the piercing of Mars, there is loss of wealth and death.<-
->Saturn causes disease and painful affliction; Rahu and Ketu are the givers of obstacles. **|| 31 ||**<-
->For the Moon, the result is said to be mixed; for Venus, there is the gain of pleasure and a beautiful body.<-
->In the piercing of Mercury, intelligence is born; Jupiter is the giver of all successful results. **|| 32 ||**<-
->Benefic and malefic planets alike destroy the name; through the name, wealth is destroyed.<-
->Through "destructive" piercings, men face ruin; through a triple piercing, there is fear for one's life. **|| 33 ||**<-

Full strength (60 units) when in one's own sign; 45 units (three-quarters) in a friend's sign; 30 units (half) in a neutral sign.
->A quarter strength is found in an enemy's sign. **|| 34 ||**<-

---

When the zodiac sign is pierced, there are great obstacles and even death. **|| 31 ||** Now the author describes the results of piercing by benefic planets. Regarding the "mixed result" of the Moon: if the Moon is pierced while associated with malefics, the result is bad; if it is a full Moon, the result of the piercing is auspicious. In the piercing of Venus <note>original: "Shukra"</note>, there is happiness in the form of sexual pleasure and play. In the piercing of Mercury <note>original: "Budha"</note>, there is wisdom—this means one gains true knowledge regarding what should be accepted and what should be rejected. In the piercing of Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note>, every result is auspicious. This means the combined result is fortunate. This is the author’s own opinion. A single piercing causes destruction or a fall from one's position, while a double piercing causes a specific defect of speech. **|| 32 ||**

Now for the specific details: regarding "benefics and malefics," if both a benefic and a malefic planet together pierce the name and strike the 23rd lunar mansion <note>term: vainashika-nakshatra; a specific 'destructive' point in Vedic astrology calculated from the birth star</note>, then there is a great destruction of wealth. The points known as the "Destructive," "Collective," and "Associative" <note>term: vainashika, samudayika, and sanghatika; technical points in the Sarvatobhadra grid used to predict specific life areas</note> are ruined, and a triple piercing results in death. **|| 33 ||** Now, the author explains whether a planet gives full or partial results based on its strength. In the verse "Strength in its own sign," one should know that a planet's strength is full when it is in its own zodiac sign, its own division, or its own time. In a friend's sign, or a friend's division or time, one should subtract one quarter—meaning its strength is three-quarters (45 units). It has half strength in a neutral sign and a quarter strength in an enemy’s sign. This is the general rule. **|| 34 ||**

->This strength of benefics and malefics is equal based on their position.<-
->One should understand these results; for benefics and malefics, the effects are reversed. **|| 35 ||**<-

Now for the specific details: This chart for benefics indicates that strength is determined by their position in their own signs. However, for malefic planets, the strength and the resulting suffering are understood in reverse. For instance, the "reversal" means that even if a malefic is in an enemy's sign, if it has full strength...

<summary>This page explains how to calculate the strength of astrological effects, noting that the intensity of a "piercing" influence (vedha) depends on whether a planet is in a friendly or its own zodiac sign. Now, the consideration is stated. Regarding the phrase "malefic planets," the word here refers to their specific position. The strength of the result should be declared according to the time. Regarding the "positional piercing" <note>term: sthana-vedha; an astrological influence exerted by a planet based on its current zodiac sign or house</note>, just as a planet in its own house, a friend's house, or even an enemy's house or a neutral house—the "piercing" is produced in that house through retrograde motion and other states. That is what is explained here. 

When a planet is in its own sign, it is "positionally strong." When a constellation <note>term: nakshatra; one of the 27 lunar mansions</note> is pierced by a planet, the result depends on which is more powerful. The combined result is a fraction of the total effect. For example, if the Sun <note>original: "Ravi"</note> is situated in Leo <note>original: "Simha"</note>—which is its own sign—and pierces a constellation, the result is achieved at three or four parts of its strength. It becomes a full quarter of the total power. If a benefic planet is in its own sign and creates a piercing, Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> gives the full result. In this way, the outcome for a malefic planet is greed, confusion of the mind, fear, and disease or death. These meanings should be stated first, indicating that fear and other misfortunes occur wherever the "piercing" hits.

## Cruel and gentle planets, whether in retrograde, moving fast, or in their highest or lowest positions, ##
## one should declare the result after understanding the "positional awakening" <note>original: "bodham"; referring to the planet's state of activity</note> and its timing. || 37 ||
## The result of a retrograde planet is doubled; when situated in its exaltation <note>term: uccha; the sign where a planet's influence is strongest</note>, it is likewise. ##
## A fast-moving planet gives its natural result; a planet in its sign of debilitation <note>term: nicha; the sign where a planet's influence is weakest</note> gives half the result. || 38 ||
## One should avoid a lunar day <note>term: tithi</note>, a zodiac sign <note>term: rashi</note>, or a constellation that is "pierced" by a malefic planet. ##
## This applies to all worldly activities, which should be avoided with great effort. || 39 ||
## One does not find joy in a marriage, and from a journey, one does not return. ##
## A sick person is not released from disease if the action is taken during the "time of the piercing strike" <note>original: "var-vela"; a specific prohibited time or 'shadow' period</note>. || 40 ||
## In the place of a "piercing," there is defeat in battle and destruction in a fort. ##
## There is also the "entry of the poet" <note>note: "kavi-pravesha"; likely a technical term for a specific astrological ingress or a signal for a commander/strategist</note> into the constellation, and the slaughter of warriors there. || 41 ||
## If a "piercing" occurs at the onset of a disease, caused by a malefic planet, ##
## death occurs if the planet is in retrograde motion; if it is fast-moving, the person remains afflicted with pain. || 42 ||

If that constellation is "pierced" by a planet in that position, results like fear should be predicted. If the planet is in its own constellation and is retrograde, the "piercing" is complete; the quality is not diminished. The result is doubled or even quadrupled. If the piercing occurs in a friend's sign, fear and other results arise, but anger and such should not be predicted. If the planet is a friend or in its exalted sign, then the results for its followers—fear and so on—should be stated. Fast-moving planets give only their natural, basic result. Regarding positional strength: in its own house or a friend's house, it is one-quarter, and so on. A planet in its sign of debilitation gives half the result. If a friendly planet is in its debilitation, it moves like a half-step or a partial foot. || 37 || And so, "the result of a retrograde planet is doubled." || 38 || Regarding the lunar day, sign, and constellation: if they are pierced by Mars <note>original: "Bhima"</note> or Saturn <note>original: "Kona"</note> or any malefic planet, all auspicious and inauspicious things should be avoided with effort; this is easy to understand. || 39 || "One does not find joy"—a marriage performed at the time of a piercing does not go well and does not lead to auspiciousness. In a journey, one does not return. If a disease begins at the time of a "piercing strike," and if a malefic piercing occurs later, the patient is not freed from the disease. || 40 || And as will be said, "at the time of disease," this is easy to understand. || 41 || "In the place of a piercing"—wherever a village or location is pierced, one should not stay there. If the village position is pierced by a strong malefic, one should not stay in that village or place. In a "pierced field," one should not wage battle. Because of that, one suffers and flees; a "pierced village" should be abandoned. One should establish and examine the *Sarvatobhadra Chakra* <note>term: Sarvatobhadra Chakra; a 9x9 square grid used for complex astrological forecasting</note> regarding the fort. If the direction, constellation, color, sign, or sound is pierced, the fort will be broken and destroyed. One should not perform the "poet's entry" into that direction or piercing. Regarding the "slaughter of warriors": after determining the strength of the warrior's constellation, if that constellation is pierced in the *Sarvatobhadra Chakra*—

->|| There, the slaughter will occur. || 42 ||<-

Thus, one should calculate the results based on the positional strength of the planet in its own house, combined with the results of the Chakra and other tools. || 53 ||

### Now, the Analysis of the Diagram is spoken.

In the Northeast <note>original: "Ishana"</note> position, starting from the East, the signs Taurus, Gemini, and Cancer <note>original: "Vrishabha, Mithuna, Kalira"</note> are located; these constellations <note>term: nakshatra; the 27 lunar mansions of the zodiac</note> are situated in the Eastern direction. Leo, Virgo, and Libra <note>original: "Simha, Kanya, Tula"</note> are situated in the Southeast <note>original: "Agni-pura"</note> and Southern directions. Scorpio, Sagittarius, and Capricorn <note>original: "Vrischika, Dhanu, Makara"</note> are situated in the East <note>note: likely a scribal error for Southwest</note> and Western directions. Aquarius, Pisces, and Aries <note>original: "Kumbha, Meena, Mesha"</note> are situated in the Northern directions. || 43 || 44 || The directions of their periods are now stated. Regarding the constellations: in the East, the vowels "Ṛ, Ū, Ṭ, U, E" <note>original: "ऋ उ ट उ ए"</note> and the seven constellations beginning with Krittika, Rohini, Mrigashirsha, Ardra, Punarvasu, Pushya, and Ashlesha are placed. This constitutes the five-fold group of the diagram. Likewise, in travel, the zodiac signs, constellations, lunar days <note>term: tithi; a lunar day in the Hindu calendar</note>, and the days of the Sun and Mars—these remain in their respective positions for a period of two to three months.

> Where the shadow begins from the East, just as the Sun <note>original: "Ravi"</note> moves through the signs beginning with Aries—that direction is considered "measured." The remaining three directions are always "risen" <note>note: "risen" (udita) refers to directions that are active or auspicious</note>. || 43 || The vowels located in the Northeast, the East, the Southwest, and the Southeast. In the Southwest, West <note>original: "Varunyam"</note>, Northwest, and North <note>original: "Somya"</note>, they are traditionally understood. || 44 || Constellations, vowels, letters, zodiac signs, lunar days, and directions: the Southwest is to be known as the "direction of the Ancestors" <note>original: "Nairuta"</note> where the Sun resides for three months. || 45 || If there is a "piercing" <note>term: vedha; an astrological alignment where a planet influences a specific point</note> in the constellation or the vowel, it brings loss and grief in a "fixed" sign <note>term: sthira-lagna; a zodiacal sign characterized by stability, such as Taurus or Leo</note>. If the zodiac sign is pierced, it causes fear; if all five elements are pierced, death is certain. || 46 || In travel, war, or marriage disputes, or in the construction of doors and palaces—men should not perform these actions while facing the "extinguished direction" <note>term: alasha; a direction rendered inauspicious by the Sun's current position</note>. || 47 || He whose name's first letter is situated in the "extinguished direction" will be defeated in all undertakings; the King in such a state is struck down by the gods. || 48 || In the siege of a fort, in punishment, or in a great battle with the four-fold army <note>note: the traditional Indian army consisting of chariots, elephants, cavalry, and infantry</note>, those desiring victory should avoid efforts when the "ascendant" sign is in that direction. || 49 ||

Thus, the constellations, vowels, letters, zodiac signs, and lunar days associated with the "risen" directions should be known. || 45 || Now, the result of being in the "extinguished direction" is spoken. Regarding the phrase "in the constellation": this result, combined with what was previously stated, arises together... <note>note: the Sanskrit here is somewhat corrupt/repetitive, but emphasizes the continuity of the ill effects</note>. This is visible from experience. || 46 || Now, the prohibition of travel toward an "extinguished" direction is stated with the phrase "In travel, war...". One should not undertake a journey facing that direction, nor should one engage in battle, or in a marriage dispute. Furthermore, the construction of a palace door or any secret matter should not be done facing the "extinguished direction." || 47 || Now, another result of the "extinguished" state is spoken with the phrase "Situated in the extinguished direction"; this is easy to understand. || 48 || Regarding "In the siege of a fort": for whichever warrior the vowels, letters, and zodiac signs are "unconnected" <note>original: "asangata"</note>, those warriors should be known as "unconnected" (vulnerable). For a warrior who is thus unconnected, what should be avoided? He should avoid fort-battles and avoid battles involving corporal punishment. Especially, he should not remain in a great battle with the four-fold army. || 49 || Following this, the result for those whose constellations, vowels, etc., are "risen" (active/favorable) is spoken. In those days, there is purification of the body and gain. When the five elements—constellation, letter, vowel, zodiac sign, and lunar day—are all "risen," one obtains a position such as a kingdom. || 50 ||

### Now, the result of "piercing" at the time of a query is spoken.

The phrase "At the time of a query" <note>original: "prashna-kale"</note> is easily understood. || 51 || Regarding the phrase "pierced by malefic planets" <note>original: "krūrair-viddhair"; malefic or "cruel" planets usually refer to the Sun, Mars, Saturn, and the waning Moon</note>: whatever result was stated for the Ascendant <note>term: lagna; the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at the time of a query</note> in its movable and other states, all those should be applied here by the teachers and astrologers. The result should be known through the specific nature of the Ascendant. If the nature of the Ascendant is considered in matters of gain or loss, victory or defeat, or lost items, one should speak the result according to the nature of the village, forest, or water-signs <note>note: specific categories of zodiac signs used to locate lost objects or people</note>.

Now, he speaks of the result stated for the movable and other types of Ascendants: If a movable sign <note>original: "chara-lagna"; Aries, Cancer, Libra, Capricorn</note> is rising, the lost object is difficult to recover and is in motion. For a person born under this, one should predict a short lifespan. If a fixed sign <note>original: "sthira-lagna"; Taurus, Leo, Scorpio, Aquarius</note> is rising, the lost item is recovered in a short time; for a sick person, the disease lasts a long time, just as for one born under it, it indicates a long life. If a dual-natured sign <note>original: "dvisvabhāva"; Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, Pisces</note> is rising, the lost item is recovered quickly and the sick person recovers quickly. For one born under this, a middle lifespan is predicted. These effects of the movable and other signs have been declared. || 52 ||

Now, another point regarding "piercing from both sides" by malefic planets: there are three types of piercing.

-> Sight upon the rising constellation <note>term: nakshatra</note> as previously stated; happiness in the vowel. Victory for the King. <-
-> In the lunar day <note>term: tithi</note>, there is luster; attainment of status in the rising of the five-fold group. || 50 || At the time of a query, <-
-> whichever Ascendant is pierced by malefic planets <note>original: "krūra-khecharaiḥ"</note>; if seen by benefics, it is auspicious; if by mixed planets, the result <-
-> will be mixed. || 51 || When the Ascendant is struck by planets, the result is according to the nature of the Ascendant. <-
-> This should be known by teachers and astrologers when the Ascendant is movable and so on. || 52 || <-
-> Pierced on both sides by malefic planets, the lunar days and vowels within it, the zodiac sign and constellation, <-
-> there is no doubt that the person dies. || 53 || A district, city, village, <-
-> a limb, a temple, or a town; if pierced on both sides by malefic planets, it is destroyed without a doubt. || 54 || <-
-> In the triad of constellations starting with Krittika, through the sequence of malefic piercing, <-
-> it destroys the countries and the people of those countries in order. || 55 || <-

It is said: the piercing is Left, Right, or Frontal. Piercing from the left is when a planet pierces through the left sight; thus there is piercing on both sides. Likewise, one pierces via the frontal sight, or there is piercing from the right sight. In whichever direction or in whichever constellation a planet with medium motion <note>original: "madhya-gati"</note> resides, if another planet with medium motion moves in the constellation directly opposite to it, then that is "frontal piercing." Thus, if one is pierced on both sides by malefic planets, and if the vowels, lunar days, and so on of a person are caught in between, then that person's death occurs just as death is described. || 53 ||

"A district or city": the phrase "pierced on both sides by malefic planets" has already been explained. This should be understood in the context of the *Krameshachakra* <note>note: a specific cyclical arrangement of the diagram</note>, and the five-fold group starting with the first letter of the name should also be considered here. || 54 ||

Now the result of piercing elsewhere is spoken: "Starting from Krittika." In the **Tortoise Diagram** <note>term: kūrma-cakra; a geographical map of India superimposed on a tortoise shape for astrological predictions</note>, if one of the three-fold groups of constellations starting with Krittika is pierced on both sides by malefic planets, then the countries divided into nine parts are destroyed. There, in the triad starting with Krittika, which is called the "navel of the tortoise," if pierced on both sides by malefics, the countries known as Mithila, Kosala, and the Kaushiki river region are destroyed. Also, Gaya, the "frontal piercing" in the Tortoise, and the malefic influence over Kanyakubja <note>note: modern Kannauj</note> and Prayag <note>note: modern Allahabad</note>—these countries are destroyed. The meaning is this: it does not mean all countries are destroyed, but rather, when the three constellations starting with Krittika are pierced, then among the countries mentioned in the "navel," whichever country's name-vowels and so on are pierced in the Five-fold Diagram, that specific country is destroyed. This is the consideration. || 55 ||

### Now, he speaks of the consideration of "piercing" in other matters.

Regarding the phrase "In Krittika," the meaning is this: the effect does not apply to everyone collectively. It affects specific regions, villages within those regions, or specific groups. In the four nakshatras <note>term: Nakshatra; a lunar mansion or sector of the sky. The series here begins with Krittika (the Pleiades)</note> starting with Krittika, one should find the "piercing" for Brahmins and other castes separately in the five-fold divisions of meat, wealth, and so on.
<margin>32</margin> However, if a "piercing" <note>term: Vedha; literally "piercing," referring to an astrological aspect where a planet's influence strikes a specific point in the diagram</note> is made by malefic planets <note>original: "krūrair"; cruel or malefic planets like the Sun, Mars, or Saturn</note>, and if that planet sees the birth sign in the Twelve-Spoked Wheel <note>term: Dvadashara Chakra; a specific astrological diagram used for predictions</note>, then the result occurs. This is explained specifically later: wherever there is destruction caused by malefic piercing, there may be a recovery or balance if a benefic planet also casts its gaze. This was proclaimed for the Brahmin class. For the Kshatriyas <note>note: the warrior and ruling caste</note>, the "piercing" is indicated by the "unconquered" positions. Similarly, for the Vaishyas <note>note: the merchant and farmer caste</note>, one should consider the mantras. Starting from the Sun's position, through Ashvini, Hasta, Ardra, and Abhijit—through these piercings, the effects on the twice-born classes <note>note: the upper three castes</note> and the artisans are known. || 56 ||

### Now, the consideration of malefic "piercing" in other matters.

> the effect of the piercing falls in order upon the classes, 
> starting with the Brahmins.

> everything reaches a state of high cost <note>original: "maharshatam"; dearness or price inflation</note> 
> wherever a malefic planet is situated.

> and the occurrence of an "Upagraha" <note>term: Upagraha; secondary or "shadow" planets that generally indicate obstacles or ill fortune</note>, 
> there is no doubt of death, 
> or perhaps severe diseases and the like.
> || 58 || The fifth nakshatra from the Sun’s position 
> is known as "Vidyunmukha" <note>gloss: Lightning-faced</note>.
> The eighth is called "Shula" <note>gloss: The Spear</note>, 
> and the fourteenth is "Sannipata" <note>gloss: Collapse/Conjunction</note>.

> The twenty-first is "Ulka" <note>gloss: The Meteor</note>. 
> The twenty-second is "Kampa" <note>gloss: The Tremor</note>.
> The twenty-third is "Vajra" <note>gloss: The Thunderbolt</note>.

> These are the "Upagrahas" spoken of by the wise. 
> They are said to cause "piercing" in their respective places 
> in all undertakings, at all times. || 61 ||

Regarding "Oil vessels": This refers to the instructions in the *Karma Chakra* <note>term: Karma Chakra; the "Wheel of Action," a specific diagnostic diagram</note>. When a piercing occurs in a region or village affecting oil vessels, molasses, and so on, everything in that place becomes very expensive—meaning it becomes difficult to obtain even with much wealth. If a benefic planet <note>original: "shubha-graha"; such as Jupiter or Venus</note> pierces, it becomes "fair-priced" <note>original: "samargha"</note>, meaning it is easily obtained with little wealth. || 57 || Regarding "Malefic piercing"... || 58 ||

### Now, he speaks of the Upagrahas.

Regarding "the fifth nakshatra from the Sun"... || 59 || Regarding "Ketu"... || 60 || And "Nirghata" is the twenty-fourth. Regarding "in their own places": the piercing is done from their own positions according to their gaze. The secondary planets like *Vidyunmukha* always cause obstacles in all works in the places where they are located. These Upagrahas pierce directly, or to the left or right, according to their position and gaze. *Vidyunmukha* is known as a source of obstacles; *Shula* is proclaimed as a thorn/pain; *Sannipata* and *Vajra* should be known as death-dealing; *Ketu* is well-known; *Ulka* is known as the teacher of death; *Vajra* is called the divider. *Kampa* is to be known as Saturn, and *Nirghata* is indeed Rahu <note>note: here the author correlates the abstract secondary points with the known malefic planets like Saturn and the North Node, Rahu</note>. In whatever manner they pierce a person, they cause those effects. These are arranged by their power in the Twelve-Spoked Wheel. || 62 ||

<summary>This page describes how malefic planets "pierce" (vedha) specific nakshatras to cause economic inflation and hardship for different social classes. It also details the calculation of the Upagrahas—eight ominous astrological points derived from the Sun's position that predict obstacles, disease, or death.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the technical exposition of Vedha (astrological "piercing") from the previous discussion on social classes. # On the Different Types of Birth Stars

Now, he speaks of another matter: the Birth Star and the Action Star. The "Birth Star" <note>original: "Janmabha"; the Nakshatra the moon occupied at the moment of birth</note> is of two kinds. First, there is the universal or collective one which concerns all people. The second is the individual birth star, which must be calculated for each person separately. Now, he explains the distinctions and demonstrates them in verse 63. Here, the star marked with one's name is the Birth Star, while the tenth star from it is designated as the "Action Star" <note>term: Karmabha; the 10th lunar mansion, which governs one's work, career, and public life</note> in verse 64. 

The eighteenth star is also mentioned in verse 65, and the sixteenth star is known as the "Conglomerate Star" in verse 66. Now, he describes the results of "piercing" <note>term: Vedha; an astrological aspect where a planet's position "strikes" or "pierces" another sensitive point in a diagram</note> for both cases, stating that "Death occurs when the Birth Star is pierced," and so on, through verse 70. 

Regarding the "Country Star": through the conjunction of secondary planets <note>term: Upagraha; mathematically derived points in a horoscope that usually have a sinister or malefic influence</note>, if the birth star is pierced, death follows; if the Action Star is pierced, there is trouble in battle, and so on. It must be said that all of this will certainly come to pass due to the conjunction of these secondary planets.

->The Birth Star is the first, and the tenth is the Action Star.<-
->The nineteenth is the Conception Star, and the twentieth is the Destruction Star. 64.<-
->The eighteenth star is known as the Collective Star.<-
->The sixteenth star here is known as the Conglomerate Star. 65.<-
->Regarding the three-fold state: there is the Caste Star for one's own kind,<-
->the Country Star for one's nation, and the Coronation Star for the throne. 66.<-
->Death occurs if the Birth Star is pierced; in the Action Star, there is distress.<-
->In the Conception Star, one goes into exile; in the Destruction Star, conflict with kin. 67.<-
->In the Collective Star, there is loss of property; likewise in the Conglomerate Star.<-
->In the Caste Star, the ruin of the family; in the Coronation Star, imprisonment. 68.<-
->In the Country Star, the ruin of the nation. Thus it is with malefics; with benefics, it is good.<-
->When a secondary planet is in conjunction, death occurs; it is not otherwise. 69.<-
->Fear, defeat, and striking; death occurs when malefic planets reside there.<-
->The result of a "war-piercing" occurs when one to five malefics strike. 70.<-

When pierced by auspicious planets <note>original: "shubha"; benefic planets like Jupiter or Venus</note>, the result is good. If pierced by both auspicious and malefic planets, the result is mixed. In the *Compilation on Castes* <note>original: "Jati-Samhita"</note>, this is written in reverse order... <note>The text here becomes technical regarding the specific groupings of Nakshatras for different species and objects</note>. These are the "Caste Stars" for those born from water, etc. The six stars starting from the Birth Star are called the "Pulse Stars" <note>term: Nadi-Nakshatras; sensitive points likened to the pulse in a body</note>. For a King, there are nine such Pulse Stars, including the three—Caste, Country, and Coronation stars. If a Pulse Star is pierced, one should not perform any religious duties <note>original: "dharma"</note>. 68. <insert>Now further</insert>. 

Regarding "Fear and Defeat": when the stars starting from the Birth Star are pierced by <insert>malefics</insert>, ranging from one to five malefic planets standing in front and causing the piercing, the following should be predicted. If pierced by one planet, predict fear. If by two, there is defeat or flight. If by three, there is conflict. If by four, there is a striking or wounding. If by five, death occurs. 70. 

Now, he explains the determination of the results for these piercings. Regarding the "Lunar Day and Star": the group of five starting from the star is auspicious if the piercing planet is benefic. On the day the Moon pierces this group of five, one should predict good fortune. If the group of five is pierced by a malefic during a Moon-piercing, one should predict misfortune on that day. 71.

<meta>This page continues the technical astrological instructions from the previous page, transitioning from the health and fate of individuals to the "determination of prices" (Argha-nirnaya). It explains how planetary "piercing" (Vedha) affects the economy, market values, and the movement of merchants and kings.</meta>

"Now I shall speak of the determination of value" — this is clear. || 72 || Now, he speaks of the determination of the "piercing" of subjects according to place and time. "Place and Time" refers to the merchants' determination of place: in which country will goods be **cheaply priced** <note>original: "samardha"</note> or **expensively priced** <note>original: "mahargha"</note>? "Commodity" <note>original: "varanam"</note> refers to the goods for sale. "Determination of time" refers to which year, season, or month goods will be cheap or expensive. This is the determination of the merchants' trade. || 73 ||

Now he explains the division of places. "Place, region, and site" — this is easy to understand. || 74 ||

"Metals, Foreign Goods, and Living Beings": **Metals** <note>original: "dhatu"</note> include gold, silver, and the like. **Foreign Goods** <note>original: "mlechha"</note> <note>The scribe uses "mralecha," a non-standard spelling for items associated with non-Vedic trade</note> include lead, tin, musk, deer, thorn-apple, and so on. **Living Beings** <note>original: "jiva"</note> include elephants, horses, and various animals.

The Lunar Day, the Star, the Sound, the Zodiac Sign, and the Weekday — these are the five factors. On whichever day the Moon is **pierced** <note>term: Vedha; an astrological aspect where a planet's position "strikes" or influences a specific point in a diagram</note>, that day will bring good or bad results. || 71 ||

Now I shall speak of the determination of prices as stated in the *Tritayamala* <note>a classic text of the Tantric or astrological tradition</note>. Good and bad results are determined by the "piercing" of planets within the **Eighty-one Square Grid** <note>term: Ekashiti-pada chakra; a 9x9 astrological chart used for forecasting economic and environmental trends</note>. || 72 ||

Place, Time, and the Commodity — these three alone are to be considered in the determination of prices. They should be known by the wise at all times. || 73 ||

Place is said to be three-fold: the Country, the Region, and the Site. Time is also described as three-fold: the Year, the Month, and the Day. || 74 ||

Commodities are considered three-fold: Metals, Foreign Goods, and Living Beings. Now I shall speak of the planetary lords of these three-fold groups. || 75 ||

Rahu, Saturn, and Jupiter are the lords of the Country. Ketu, the Sun, and Venus rule the Site. Mars and Mercury sustain the [other] places. || 76 ||

Because merchants, for the sake of profit and according to their own ability, must sell or buy goods in various <gloss>places</gloss>, they must consider: "In which country or at what time will metals and other goods become cheap or expensive?" Or, Kings and others may ask: "In which place and at what time should I build a great fort?" Others may ask: "To which country and at what time should I go for my livelihood, or what work should I do?" There are many such various options. || 75 ||

Now he speaks of the lords of the three-fold divisions of places and others. "The lords of the country are Rahu and Saturn..." Rahu, Saturn, and Jupiter are the lords of the country. Just as "Country" refers to a holy pilgrimage site, "Region" refers to various territories, and "Site" refers to a specific plot of land. Here, the lords of the country are spoken of generally as the lords of all lands, according to the zodiac sign of the country. Similarly, for the Site and Time, the lords of the Region are Ketu, the Sun, and Venus. The lords of the Site are Mars and Mercury. || 76 ||

Now he speaks of the lords of the three-fold Time: "The lords of the Year..." || 77 ||

"The lords of Metals are Saturn..." <note>original: "Sauri"</note>. Here, the lords are assigned by their inherent nature, not by their connection to a star or zodiac sign. || 78 ||

"The Five Gates" — this is for general purposes, not according to inherent nature. || 79 ||
